[SWAPWEAP]
@bind CTRL-2
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equip two-hander

Macro for swap between dual one hand to shield+one hand:
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equipslot [worn:shield,noworn:two-hand] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [worn:shield] 17 one-off-hand
Note: When making this macro you'll need to replace one-hander, shield, and two-hander with the names of the weapons that your character is specifically using. For instance, if you were using Obsidian Edged Blade you would write Obsidian Edged Blade instead of two-hander
[shield] must be localized to your native language too!!!
 IconID: 132996, spellID: 20219

[3->4] meta slots

[SWAPWEAP2]
@bind CTRL-2
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equip two-hander

Macro for swap between dual one hand to shield+one hand:
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equipslot [worn:shield,noworn:two-hand] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [worn:shield] 17 one-off-hand
Note: When making this macro you'll need to replace one-hander, shield, and two-hander with the names of the weapons that your character is specifically using. For instance, if you were using Obsidian Edged Blade you would write Obsidian Edged Blade instead of two-hander
[shield] must be localized to your native language too!!!
 IconID: 132996, spellID: 20219

[SWAPWEAP3]
@bind CTRL-2
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equip two-hander

Macro for swap between dual one hand to shield+one hand:
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equipslot [worn:shield,noworn:two-hand] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [worn:shield] 17 one-off-hand
Note: When making this macro you'll need to replace one-hander, shield, and two-hander with the names of the weapons that your character is specifically using. For instance, if you were using Obsidian Edged Blade you would write Obsidian Edged Blade instead of two-hander
[shield] must be localized to your native language too!!!
 IconID: 132996, spellID: 20219

[3->4] meta slots

[PETCOMMA]
@bind CTRL-P
/cast [pet:observer,@mouseover,harm][pet:observer]Optical Blast;[pet:wrathguard,@mouseover,harm][pet:wrathguard]Axe Toss;[pet:imp,@mouseover,help][pet:imp,@focus,help][pet:felhunter/incubus/succubus,@mouseover,harm][]Command Demon

Note: wrathguard, imp, voidwalker, felhunter, observer, incubus, succubus, felguard must be translated into your native language

Singe Magic
Shadow Bulwark
Spell Lock
Optical Blast
Seduction
Axe Tosss
